- **chargeblast-credits**: Chargeblast credit specialist: when an alert earns a credit (automatically_request_credit=true with eligible response codes, the 7 day window, no RDR, no previous request, not bill-per-resolved pricing), which codes are subject to review, the credit request endpoint and its failure cases, why Resolved alerts go through support, and why the flag must accompany eligible codes. - **chat**: This document is written **for AI agents**. SystemView is the IDE in the browser, and its chat panel attaches to **your live session**. When the human types there, he is talking to you *in this conversation* — not into a room you have to enter, and not through a relay. Your reply **is** the message. Siblings: [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md), [markdown.md](markdown.md). ([Odion100/systemview](https://github.com/Odion100/systemview/blob/HEAD/agents/chat.md))
- **chat**: Goal operator chat — a conversational assistant scoped to one goal. Inspects board state, drafts tasks with full instructions, refines goal context, and triggers the planner. Runs one turn at a time; uses a PROPOSE_ACTION protocol so the parent server can gate destructive operations behind human confirm. ([pblvrt/helix](https://github.com/pblvrt/helix/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chat.md))

- **checker_ambiguous**: Phase-2 checker for the ambiguous category (amber, #FFBF00). Identifies statements whose meaning is unclear, underspecified, or admits multiple plausible readings that change the paper's conclusions. Runs in parallel with the other checker_* agents. FLAGGED requires presenting two distinct interpretations and explaining why the ambiguity matters. ([doloresgarcia/Anderson](https://github.com/doloresgarcia/Anderson/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/checker_ambiguous.md))
- **checker_contradiction**: Phase-2 checker for the internal_contradiction category (orange, #FF6D00). Identifies pairs of statements within the paper that cannot both be true (conflicting numbers, abstract↔results mismatches, method↔evaluation incompatibilities). Runs in parallel with the other checker_* agents. Both contradicting passages must be quoted with paper.txt provenance. ([doloresgarcia/Anderson](https://github.com/doloresgarcia/Anderson/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/checker_contradiction.md))
- **checker_domain**: Phase-2 checker for the domain_violation category (purple, #7B1FA2). Identifies statements that contradict basic, established knowledge any expert in the paper's field would recognize as wrong. Runs in parallel with the other checker_* agents. FLAGGED requires stating the violated principle and naming a canonical source. Debated positions are not violations. ([doloresgarcia/Anderson](https://github.com/doloresgarcia/Anderson/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/checker_domain.md))
- **checker_literature**: Phase-2 checker for the literature_collision category (red, #D32F2F). For each claim with candidates in LITERATURE.md, verifies whether the paper's statement directly conflicts with the cited source — reading bank PDFs and falling back to WebFetch/WebSearch for external references. FLAGGED requires citing both the paper passage and the contradicting source with snippet, with a real bibtex key in references.bib. ([doloresgarcia/Anderson](https://github.com/doloresgarcia/Anderson/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/checker_literature.md))
- **checker_unreferenced**: Phase-2 checker for the unreferenced category (blue, #4285F4). Identifies claims that assert a fact requiring a citation but provide none. Runs in parallel with the other four checker_* agents. Writes its section to its own working dir; the orchestrator concatenates into reviews/&lt;slug&gt;/phase2/outputs/VERIFICATION.md. Does NOT flag the paper's own novel results. ([doloresgarcia/Anderson](https://github.com/doloresgarcia/Anderson/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/checker_unreferenced.md))

- **chef**: Chef de Cuisine — the coordinator/planner of the kitchen brigade. Takes a request and drives it to a reviewed artifact (strategy, implementation plan, diagnosis, or a parallel workflow). Plans the menu, expedites the line, and owns the pass. Lean, stack-agnostic equivalent of the muppets' kermit. ([avegancafe/kitchen-brigade](https://github.com/avegancafe/kitchen-brigade/blob/HEAD/agents/chef.md))
- **chef**: Chef, a Harvest Sprite on the implementer crew. Implements one small, independently verifiable chunk of an approved plan, test first, inside the permitted files only. Use proactively for each chunk once the plan is approved. ([belajarcarabelajar/vivera](https://github.com/belajarcarabelajar/vivera/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chef.md))
- **chef**: Scoped Jiro chef for implementation and review. Loads jiro before working, follows the parent's brief, and returns changes with evidence. ([kyeshmz/jiro](https://github.com/kyeshmz/jiro/blob/HEAD/agents/chef.md))
- **chef**: Synthesis agent. Cross-references findings from multiple gather agents into one coherent report. Use when cowboy needs to consolidate multi-agent investigation outputs into actionable recommendations. ([socalfinanceinsights/claude-os](https://github.com/socalfinanceinsights/claude-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chef.md))
- **Chef d'orchestre**: Coordonne l'Analyste PO, le Développeur et le Testeur QA pour livrer une fonctionnalité TaskFlow de bout en bout (bonus de l'Atelier 9). ([houssemeddinelassoued/my-deck](https://github.com/houssemeddinelassoued/my-deck/blob/HEAD/labs/copilot-debutant/corriges/ateliers/atelier-09-agents/.github/agents/chef-orchestre.agent.md) · ★1)
- **chef-clinique-biboumed**: Médecin généraliste, chef de la clinique médicale. Analyse les dossiers médicaux, recherche les symptômes/maladies/médicaments et répond aux patients. ([Gsyltc/homelab-portfolio](https://github.com/Gsyltc/homelab-portfolio/blob/HEAD/temporaire/agents/chef-clinique-biboumed.md) · ★1)
- **chef-cuisine**: Chef de cuisine, expert fiches techniques et production. Utiliser pour les grammages, coefficients de cuisson, fiches techniques, mise en place, allergènes, anti-gaspillage et l'organisation de la production. ([PrendsTaPart/Plugin-Claude-MCP-BraindCode-](https://github.com/PrendsTaPart/Plugin-Claude-MCP-BraindCode-/blob/HEAD/foodeatup/agents/chef-cuisine.md) · ★8)
- **chef-cuisinier-clinique-biboumed**: Chef cuisinier de la clinique Biboumed : planifie des repas équilibrés réalisables avec les appareils de cuisine disponibles (décrits dans la skill recherche-repas), propose 3 suggestions par plat à valider par l'humain, re-propose un autre plat après tout refus, diversifie aléatoirement les sources de recettes, établit un calendrier des repas à partir du prochain lundi (validation humaine keep/modify) et crée les évènements dans l'agenda, conserve l'emplacement de chaque recette, journalise les repas retenus (historique glissant 30 jours), gère les exclusions (repas et ingrédients refusés),… ([Gsyltc/homelab-portfolio](https://github.com/Gsyltc/homelab-portfolio/blob/HEAD/temporaire/agents/chef-cuisinier-clinique-biboumed.md) · ★1)
- **chef-d-equipe**: Un agent à qui tu parles, qui crée, nomme et pilote les autres agents à ta place. ([jahid611/orbit-ide](https://github.com/jahid611/orbit-ide/blob/HEAD/extensions/orbit/control/guide/agents/chef-d-equipe.md))
- **chef-d-orchestre**: Découpe une grosse tâche en sous-tâches indépendantes, lance un agent par sous-tâche, puis fusionne leur travail. ([jahid611/orbit-ide](https://github.com/jahid611/orbit-ide/blob/HEAD/extensions/orbit/control/guide/agents/chef-d-orchestre.md))
- **chef-de-cuisine**: Executive chef. Owns the menu (strategy), the prep list (implementation plan), and runs the pass during service (execution). Drives AI review loops with sous-chef and human-review gates via contact_supervisor. Modal across stages — the chain step body picks MENU / PREP-LIST / SERVICE mode. ([avegancafe/Juliet](https://github.com/avegancafe/Juliet/blob/HEAD/symlinked/home/.pi/agent/agents/chef-de-cuisine.md) · ★6)
